```

Let's write helper functions.

Detailed helper specs:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    M=2*n-1
    N=next_power_of_two(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N, n=M)  # irfft with n parameter? `np.fft.irfft(v, n=M)` returns length M. But if N is even, yes. Use `[:M]` for safety.
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out[out<0]=0.0
    return out

def corr_pair(a,w):
    a=np.asarray...; w=...
    n=a.size; m=w.size
    if n==0: return empty
    if m==0: return zeros(n)
    M=n+m-1
    N=next_power_of_two(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full=np.fft.irfft(A*W, N)[:M]
    res=full[n-1:2*n-1]
    res=np.nan_to_num(res)
    res[res<0]=0.0
    return res
```
Check `np.fft.irfft(A*A,N, n=M)`? Actually if N is transform length, irfft returns length N by default. We want first M. `full = np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:M]`. Good.

`resample_sequence(a, n)`: linear interpolation. For positive. If m != n. Use `np.interp`.

Let's refine exact subgradient polish. It can improve high beta local. Use current best z. For subgrad, we can also use beta-softmax weights instead of top-k. Since exact max objective non-smooth; using a small number active. Code:
```
def subgrad_polish_z(zbest, deadline, k_list=(1,2,3,5,8,12), rates=(0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1)):
    z_cur=zbest.copy(); best_z=zbest.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_x(z_to_x(z_cur))
    current_sc=best_sc
    n=len(z_cur)
    it=0
    max_iters=200
    while it<max_iters and time<deadline:
       x=z_to_x(z_cur); c=fast_self_conv(x); d=n*c; dmax=max
       # active indices top k? For each k, create w
       any_improve=False
       for k in k_list:
           if k>=d.size: k=d.size
           idx=np.argpartition(d, d.size-k)[d.size-k:]
           w=np.zeros(d.size); w[idx]=1.0/k
           h=corr_pair(x,w); g=dot(w,c)
           grad=4*n*x*(h-g)
           gn=np.max(np.abs(grad)); if gn<=1e-12: continue
           step=grad/gn
           for eta in rates:
               z_trial=z_cur - eta*step
               # z_trial mean maybe unchanged (step sum zero), recenter
               z_trial=z_trial - z_trial.mean()
               x_trial=z_to_x(z_trial)
               sc=exact_score_x(x_trial)
               if finite and sc < best_sc:
                   best_sc=sc; best_z=z_trial.copy(); any_improve=True
               if finite and sc < current_sc:
                   current_sc=sc; z_cur=z_trial.copy()
       it+=1
       if not any_improve and it>8: break
    return best_sc,best_z
```
This loops many exact evals: k_list 6 * rates 6 =36 per iter, each FFT exact ~0.1 ->3.6s per iter; 100 iters 360s. Perhaps too much. Reduce: k_list (1,2,3,5,8), rates (0.005,0.02,0.05,0.1) ->20 evals 2s/iter. 50 iter 100s. Good. We can also stop if no improvement for 3 iters. Use deadline.

Could include softmax weight step with beta=10000 in subgrad: w = softmax of top? But L-BFGS did.

def short_optimize(z, n, deadline, est, stage_list):
    z_cur=z; best_sc=exact; best_z=z
    # maybe run exact subgradient at end? no

    return

Implement:
```
def short_lbfgs(z, n, deadline, est, betas=(5000,50000,200000)):
    z_cur=z.copy(); best_sc=...; best_z=z
    pos=0
    for beta in betas:
       rem=deadline-time
       if rem<20: break
       frac=1.0/(len(betas)-pos)
       sub_dead=time.monotonic()+frac*rem
       pos+=1
       while time < sub_dead -3:
           rem_s=sub_dead-time
           mf=int(min(600, max(20, (0.5*rem_s)/est)))
           sc,z2=run_lbfgs_z(z_cur,beta,sub_dead,mf,est)
           update; if no change? break maybe
     # after each beta break if no improve? continue
    return
```

   sub_dead=t0 + (cum+frac)*total
   sub_dead=min(sub_dead, deadline-5)
   cum += frac
   while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 3:
       rem=sub_dead-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 8: break
       mf=int(min(3000, max(20, (0.6*rem)/max(est,1e-5))))
       sc,z2=run_lbfgs_z(z_cur, beta, sub_dead, mf, est)
       if np.isfinite(sc):
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=sc; best_z=z2.copy()
             no_improve_calls=0
          else:
             no_improve_calls +=1
          # update current
          if sc <= best_sc + stage_tol:
             z_cur=z2.copy()
          else:
             z_cur=best_z.copy()
       else:
          z_cur=best_z.copy()
       # if no_improve_calls > 3 and beta > 5000: maybe break? Could waste. Limit: if no_improve_calls>4 and rem>100: break stage? Then use leftover later? Since sub_dead absolute, breaking leaves time for later phases if their deadlines later. Good.
       if no_improve_calls > 5 and float(beta) > 100.0:
           # reduce tolerance? maybe break this beta stage
           break
   # after stage, maybe re-anchor current to best if far
   sc_cur=exact_score_x(z_to_x(z_cur))
   if not np.isfinite(sc_cur) or sc_cur > best_sc + 0.01:
       z_cur=best_z.copy()
# final maybe one high beta maxfun short
return best_sc,best_z

`perturb_z` top-level:
```
def perturb_z(z, rng, strength=None):
    n=len(z)
    if strength is None: strength=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.15))
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
    x=z_to_x(z)
    step=...
    if mode0: q=x*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n)); z_new=log(q+eps)
    elif mode1: t=rng.uniform(0.05,0.25); q=(1-t)*x+t/n; z_new=log
    elif mode2: z_new=z[::-1].copy() + 0.05*normal
    elif mode3: # gradient-like: compute d top3
       c=fast_self_conv(x); d=n*c; top3; w; h; g; grad=4n*x*(h-g); gn=max; z_new=z - strength*0.2*grad/gn
    elif mode4: smooth noise in z: noise = rng.standard_normal(n); smooth with Gaussian kernel; z_new=z+0.5*strength*noise
    else: # reverse blend
    z_new = z_new - mean
    return nan_to_num
```
Need Gaussian smoothing kernel top-level? Can inline. No closures.

Let's expand: ∫0^t 1 + Asin a + Asin b + A² sin a sin b. sin b = sin(2πt - 2πs)= sin2πt cos2πs - cos2πt sin2πs. sin a sin b =0.5[cos(4πs-2πt)-cos2πt]. Integrals can be done.
For A=1, compute q(t) for t∈[0,1]:
I1=t
I_sin_a = ∫0^t sin2πs ds = (1-cos2πt)/(2π)
I_sin_b = ∫ sin(2πt-2πs) ds = same = (1-cos2πt)/(2π)

Detailed code snippets:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

`add_shape`:
```
def add_shape(arr, out, cap, n):
    if len(out) >= cap:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    if arr.size != n:
        arr = resample_sequence(arr, n)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    mn = float(np.min(arr))
    if mn < 0.0:
        arr = arr - mn + 0.05
    mx = float(np.max(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    x = normalize_a(arr)
    if np.all(np.isnan(x)):
        return
    out.append(x)
```

`make_initial_starts`:
```
def make_initial_starts(n, parent, rng, cap=28):
    out=[]
    if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
       add_shape(parent,out,cap,n)
       add_shape(parent[::-1],out,cap,n)
    # uniform
    add_shape(np.ones(n),out,cap,n)
    xg=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    # powers
    pvals=(0.0,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0)
    for p in pvals:
       for q in (0.0,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0):
           arr=(xg+1e-9)**p * ((1.0-xg)+1e-9)**q
           add_shape(arr,out,cap,n)
           if len(out)>=cap: break
       if len(out)>=cap: break
    # cos modulation
    for f in (1,2,3,4):
       for amp in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
          phase=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
          arr=1.0+amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*xg+phase)
          add_shape(arr,out,cap,n)
    # abs sine / v
    for amp in (0.5,1.0,1.5):
       add_shape(1.0+amp*np.abs(2.0*xg-1.0),out,cap,n)
       add_shape(1.0+amp*(2.0*xg-1.0)**2,out,cap,n)
    # random low-pass
    Nf=next_power_of_two(n)
    freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)*Nf
    for _ in range(8):
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(1.2,3.5))
       power=1.0/((np.abs(freqs)+1.0)**alpha)
       if power.size>0: power[0]=0.0
       phase=rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,size=power.size)
       spec=power*np.exp(1j*phase)
       noise=np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n].astype(np.float64)
       noise = noise - float(np.mean(noise))
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.5,1.5))
       arr=0.5+amp*noise
       add_shape(arr,out,cap,n)
    return out
```
Need if cap reached before loops okay.

`z_from_x`:
```
def z_from_x(x):
    x=normalize_a(np.asarray(x,float).ravel())
    n=x.size
    floor = 1e-9 / float(n)
    x = np.maximum(x, floor)
    # renormalize after floor? Sum slightly >1, okay
    x = x / float(x.sum())
    z = np.log(x)
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    return np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
```
Floor 1e-9/n ~1e-13 for 1e4; log -30. Good.

`exact_score_a`:
```
def exact_score_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    a=np.nan_to_num(a,...)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*a.size*cmax/(S*S))
```
`exact_score_x(x)` can call `exact_score_a` after normalize? To avoid overhead? x sum1. Use direct:
```
def exact_score_x(x):
   x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
   if x.size==0: return inf
   x=np.nan_to_num(x); x=np.clip(x,0,None)
   S=float(x.sum()); if S<1e-12 return inf
   c=fast_self_conv(x); cmax=...
   return 2*n*cmax/(S*S)

`value_grad_lse_z`: Need if n large, beta float. Use local variables. To improve speed, avoid `z_to_x` calling nan_to_num? It's okay.
```
def value_grad_lse_z(z, beta):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0: return (np.inf, np.empty(0))
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    mx = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
       z = np.zeros_like(z); mx=0.0
    e = np.exp(z - mx)
    ss = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
       x = np.full(z.size, 1.0/float(z.size))
    else:
       x = e / ss
    n = int(x.size)
    c = fast_self_conv(x)
    if c.size == 0: return np.inf, np.zeros(n)
    # x sum should be 1; ensure
    Sx = float(x.sum())
    if Sx <= 0 or not finite: return ...
    # use normalized x? x already normalized. If not, normalize? exp normalization sums 1.
    d = float(n) * c  # since sum x=1
    # If want safety: d = n*c/(Sx*Sx) but c from x normalized. Keep.
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0:
       # flat fallback
       # maybe use small uniform weights? gradient zero? Return large? 
       val = 1.0 + 1.0/float(n)
       return float(val), np.zeros(n)
    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1e-8:
       beta = 1.0
    m = int(d.size)
    # zeta
    zeta = beta * (d - dmax)
    # clip for exp; but if beta huge and d-dmax negative large, set -745
    zeta = np.clip(zeta, -745.0, 700.0)
    ez = np.exp(zeta)
    sum_e = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sum_e) or sum_e <= 0.0:
       # fallback weights uniform active one?
       w = np.ones(m)/float(m)
       log_sum_correction = 0.0
       val = dmax  # rough
    else:
       log_m = float(np.log(float(m)))
       val = dmax + (float(np.log(sum_e)) - log_m) / beta
       w = ez / sum_e
    h = corr_pair(x, w)
    # g = dot(w,c) if c sum=1; if c sum not 1 due? use d: g = dot(w,d)/n
    g = float(np.dot(w, c))
    # if c sum not 1, g okay? c sum Sx^2 ~1. maybe use (dot(w,d)/n)
    g = float(np.dot(w, d))/float(n)
    score = 2.0 * val if np.isfinite(val) else 2.0*dmax
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * x * (h - g)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    gm = float(np.mean(grad))
    if np.isfinite(gm): grad = grad - gm
    return float(score), grad
```
Note `val` maybe less than 1? For beta small ~1. score ~1. If dmax 0 (zero sequence), return. Good.

`c` from x sum1, sum c=1, g=dot(w,c). Use that.

Potential issue: If beta high and dmax is 0? no.

`run_lbfgs_z`: Before minimize, maybe if exact score is inf, return. Use options.
```
def run_lbfgs_z(z0, beta, deadline, maxfun, est):
    z0=np.asarray(z0,float).ravel()
    if z0.size==0: return np.inf,z0
    z0=np.nan_to_num(...); z0=z0-z0.mean()
    sc0=exact_score_x(z_to_x(z0))
    if not np.isfinite(sc0): sc0=1e10; z0=np.zeros(z0.size)
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
       return sc0,z0
    maxfun=int(max(20, min(maxfun, int((0.6*(deadline-time.monotonic()))/max(est,1e-5)))))
    if maxfun < 20: return sc0,z0
    opts={'maxfun':maxfun, 'maxiter':max(50,2*maxfun), 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxcor':20, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False}
    z1=z0
    try:
        res=minimize(value_grad_lse_z, z0, args=(float(beta),), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=opts)
        if res is not None and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
            z1=np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel()
    except Exception:
        pass
    z1=np.nan_to_num(z1,...); z1=z1-z1.mean()
    sc1=exact_score_x(z_to_x(z1))
    if not np.isfinite(sc1) or sc1>1e8:
        return sc0,z0
    return float(sc1), z1
```
The `maxfun` cap based on rem; if maxfun huge, line search maybe. We cap maybe 5000? Add `maxfun = min(maxfun, 5000)` to avoid one call >500s if est underestimated. Use 4000.

`optimize_lbfgs_trajectory`: Need if `deadline` less than now+20 return.
```
def optimize_lbfgs_trajectory(zstart, n, deadline, est, stage_list, label=''):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    z_cur=np.asarray(zstart,float).copy()
    z_cur=np.nan_to_num(...); z_cur=z_cur-z_cur.mean()
    best_z=z_cur.copy()
    best_sc=exact_score_x(z_to_x(z_cur))
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc): best_sc=1e10
    total=max(0.0, deadline-t0)
    if total < 20: return best_sc,best_z
    cum=0.0
    for beta,frac in stage_list:
        sub_dead = t0 + (cum+frac)*total
        cum += frac
        if sub_dead > deadline: sub_dead=deadline
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, deadline-5.0)
        if time.monotonic() >= sub_dead - 6.0:
            continue
        no_imp=0
        tol = 0.02 if float(beta) < 50 else (0.005 if float(beta) < 1000 else 0.001)
        while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 4.0:
            rem = sub_dead - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 8.0: break
            mf = int((0.55*rem)/max(est,1e-5))
            mf = max(20, min(4000, mf))
            sc,z2 = run_lbfgs_z(z_cur, float(beta), sub_dead, mf, est)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = float(sc); best_z = z2.copy(); no_imp=0
                else:
                    no_imp += 1
                if sc <= best_sc + tol:
                    z_cur = z2.copy()
                else:
                    z_cur = best_z.copy()
            else:
                z_cur = best_z.copy()
                no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 4 and float(beta) > 200.0:
                break
        # reanchor
        sc_cur = exact_score_x(z_to_x(z_cur))
        if not np.isfinite(sc_cur) or sc_cur > best_sc + 0.01:
            z_cur = best_z.copy()
    print(label + " optimized best={:.8f}".format(best_sc))
    return float(best_sc), best_z
```
If beta high no_imp break early; time left until next stage deadline may be idle? The while breaks, but stage loop continues; if next stage deadline is later, it can run. Good. If last stage breaks, function returns before deadline; caller phases still have later deadlines? We call phases sequentially with absolute deadlines; if optimize returns early, we proceed next phase possibly before its scheduled start but will run until its deadline. Good.

def short_lbfgs(zstart, n, deadline, est, betas, label=''):
    z_cur=... best...
    pos=0
    for beta in betas:
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 20: break
       frac = 1.0 / float(len(betas)-pos)
       sub_dead = time.monotonic() + frac*rem - 5.0
       if sub_dead < time.monotonic()+15: sub_dead=time.monotonic()+15
       sub_dead=min(sub_dead, deadline-3)
       no=0
       while time.monotonic()<sub_dead-3 and no<3:
           rem_s=sub_dead-time
           if rem_s<8: break
           mf=int((0.5*rem_s)/max(est,1e-5)); mf=max(20,min(1000,mf))
           sc,z2=run_lbfgs_z(z_cur,beta,sub_dead,mf,est)
           no+=1
           update; z_cur = z2 if sc <= best+0.005 else best
       pos+=1
    return best
```

def subgrad_polish_z(zstart, deadline, k_list=(1,2,3,5,8), rate_list=(0.005,0.02,0.05,0.1)):

        improved=False

def perturb_z(z, rng, strength=None):
    n=z.size
    if strength is None: strength=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.12))
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
    z=np.asarray(z,float).copy(); z=np.nan_to_num(z); z=z-z.mean()
    x=z_to_x(z)
    if mode==0:
       arr=x*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
       z_new=z_from_x(arr)
    elif mode==1:
       t=float(rng.uniform(0.03,0.2))
       arr=(1.0-t)*x+t/float(n)
       z_new=z_from_x(arr)
    elif mode==2:
       z_new=z[::-1].copy() + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif mode==3:
       c=fast_self_conv(x); d=n*c; k=3; idx=...; w; h; g; grad=...; gn=max; z_new=z - (0.2*strength)*grad/gn? Since strength 0.05, step 0.01
    elif mode==4:
       noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
       # smooth with small kernel
       klen=max(3, int(n//500)); if even +1; klen=min(klen,n if n%2 else n-1)
       c=klen//2; sig=max(1.0,c/2.0); kern=np.exp(-((np.arange(klen)-c)**2)/(2*sig*sig)); kern/=kern.sum()
       noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,mode='same'); noise-=mean
       z_new=z + 0.5*strength*noise
    else:
       arr=x* (1.0 + 0.1*rng.standard_normal(n)); z_new=z_from_x(np.maximum(arr,1e-12))
    z_new=np.nan_to_num(z_new); z_new=z_new-z_new.mean(); return z_new
```

def is_distinct(a,b):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); b=np.asarray...
    if a.size!=b.size: return True
    d=np.max(np.abs(a-b))
    return d > 1e-4
```

    ncast=int(max(1,n))
    N1=next_power_of_two(2*ncast-1)

`estimate_eval_time`: implement cost. Let's write:
```
def estimate_eval_time(n):
    n=int(max(16,n))
    N1=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    N2=next_power_of_two(3*n-2)
    c1=float(N1)*(1.0+float(np.log2(N1)))
    c2=float(N2)*(1.0+float(np.log2(N2)))
    base=32768.0*16.0
    est=0.075*(c1+c2)/(2.0*base)
    if est<0.03: est=0.03
    if est>0.5: est=0.5
    return est
```
For n=10240 c1=524288, c2=524288, base=524288, est=0.075. Actual maybe 0.06-0.1. Good. For n=16384 c1=524288,c2=1,114,112 total1.638M /1,048,576=1.562*0.075=0.117. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(full convolution(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Main improvements:
- Optimize in log-softmax variables, which enforces positivity and scale
  invariance and gives a better-conditioned projected gradient.
- Smoothed max objective using a stable centered log-sum-exp. Small beta
  flattens the convolution, large beta approaches the true max.
- Active-set KKT-inspired flatness phase: for active diagonals, drive the
  weighted cross-correlation vector to be as flat as possible.
- Exact top-k subgradient polish, short high-beta LSE runs, perturbations,
  and multi-length exploration.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def corr_pair(a, w):
    """
    Returns h_i = sum_j a_j w_{i+j}, i=0..len(a)-1.
    No negative clipping is applied here because it is used with signed vectors.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    M = n + m - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:M]
    out = full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return out


def normalize_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    return a


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(),
                      nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def z_to_x(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(z.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    mx = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    e = np.exp(z - mx)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def z_from_x(x):
    x = normalize_a(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    floor = 1e-9 / float(n)
    x = np.maximum(x, floor)
    x = x / float(x.sum())
    z = np.log(x)
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    return np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def exact_score_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s * s))


def exact_score_x(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    c = fast_self_conv(x)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s * s))


def value_grad_lse_z(z, beta):
    """
    Score surrogate and gradient with respect to logit variables z.
    x = softmax(z), d_k = n*c_k.
    Objective:
        2 * (1/beta) log(sum_k exp(beta*d_k)) - constant,
    which behaves like mean(d) + O(beta*variance) for small beta
    and like 2*max(d) for large beta.
    """
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(z.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = z - float(z.mean())

    mxz = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mxz):
        x = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        e = np.exp(z - mxz)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
            x = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            x = e / se

    c = fast_self_conv(x)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # x from softmax is sum 1 in floating point; normalize defensively.
    sx = float(x.sum())
    if sx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sx):
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x / sx
    c = c / (sx * sx)

    d = float(n) * c
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    m = int(d.size)

    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        return 1.0 + 1.0 / float(n), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1e-8:
        beta = 10.0

    zeta = beta * (d - dmax)
    zeta = np.clip(zeta, -745.0, 700.0)
    ez = np.exp(zeta)
    sum_ez = float(ez.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(sum_ez) or sum_ez <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        val = dmax
    else:
        log_m = float(np.log(float(m)))
        val = dmax + (float(np.log(sum_ez)) - log_m) / beta
        w = ez / sum_ez

    h = corr_pair(x, w)
    g = float(np.dot(w, c))
    score = 2.0 * val if np.isfinite(val) else 2.0 * dmax

    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * x * (h - g)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    gm = float(np.mean(grad))
    if np.isfinite(gm):
        grad = grad - gm

    return float(score), grad


def estimate_eval_time(n):
    n = int(max(16, n))
    n1 = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    n2 = next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)
    c1 = float(n1) * (1.0 + float(np.log2(n1)))
    c2 = float(n2) * (1.0 + float(np.log2(n2)))
    base = 32768.0 * 16.0
    est = 0.075 * (c1 + c2) / (2.0 * base)
    if est < 0.03:
        est = 0.03
    if est > 0.5:
        est = 0.5
    return float(est)


def run_lbfgs_z(z0, beta, deadline, maxfun, est):
    z0 = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(z0.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, z0

    z0 = np.nan_to_num(z0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z0 = z0 - float(z0.mean())
    sc0 = exact_score_x(z_to_x(z0))
    if not np.isfinite(sc0):
        sc0 = 1e10
        z0 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now >= float(deadline) or maxfun < 20:
        return float(sc0), z0

    rem = float(deadline) - now
    mf = int((0.55 * rem) / max(est, 1e-5))
    mf = max(20, min(4000, mf))

    opts = {
        "maxfun": mf,
        "maxiter": max(50, 2 * mf),
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxcor": 20,
        "maxls": 20,
        "disp": False,
    }

    z1 = z0
    try:
        res = minimize(value_grad_lse_z, z0, args=(float(beta),),
                       method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, options=opts)
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            z1 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        pass

    z1 = np.nan_to_num(z1, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z1 = z1 - float(z1.mean())

    sc1 = exact_score_x(z_to_x(z1))
    if not np.isfinite(sc1) or sc1 > 1e8:
        return float(sc0), z0

    return float(sc1), z1


def get_beta_stages(score):
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < 1.52:
        # Already close: emphasize high-beta polishing.
        return [
            (50.0, 0.05),
            (300.0, 0.10),
            (1000.0, 0.15),
            (5000.0, 0.20),
            (20000.0, 0.25),
            (80000.0, 0.25),
        ]
    return [
        (10.0, 0.05),
        (50.0, 0.10),
        (200.0, 0.12),
        (1000.0, 0.15),
        (5000.0, 0.15),
        (20000.0, 0.15),
        (80000.0, 0.18),
    ]


def optimize_lbfgs_trajectory(zstart, n, deadline, est, stage_list, label=""):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    z_cur = np.asarray(zstart, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    z_cur = np.nan_to_num(z_cur, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z_cur = z_cur - float(z_cur.mean())

    best_z = z_cur.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_x(z_to_x(z_cur))
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 1e10

    total = max(0.0, float(deadline) - t0)
    if total < 20.0:
        return float(best_sc), best_z

    cumulative = 0.0
    for beta, frac in stage_list:
        sub_dead = t0 + (cumulative + float(frac)) * total
        cumulative += float(frac)
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, float(deadline) - 5.0)

        if time.monotonic() >= sub_dead - 6.0:
            continue

        no_imp = 0
        if float(beta) < 50.0:
            tol = 0.02
        elif float(beta) < 1000.0:
            tol = 0.005
        else:
            tol = 0.001

        while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 4.0:
            rem = sub_dead - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 8.0:
                break
            mf = int((0.55 * rem) / max(est, 1e-5))
            mf = max(20, min(4000, mf))
            sc, z2 = run_lbfgs_z(z_cur, float(beta), sub_dead, mf, est)

            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_z = z2.copy()
                    no_imp = 0
                else:
                    no_imp += 1

                if sc <= best_sc + tol:
                    z_cur = z2.copy()
                else:
                    z_cur = best_z.copy()
            else:
                z_cur = best_z.copy()
                no_imp += 1

            if no_imp >= 4 and float(beta) > 200.0:
                break

        sc_cur = exact_score_x(z_to_x(z_cur))
        if not np.isfinite(sc_cur) or sc_cur > best_sc + 0.01:
            z_cur = best_z.copy()

    print("{} optimized best={:.8f}".format(label, best_sc))
    return float(best_sc), best_z


def short_lbfgs(zstart, n, deadline, est, betas, label=""):
    z_cur = np.asarray(zstart, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    z_cur = np.nan_to_num(z_cur, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z_cur = z_cur - float(z_cur.mean())

    best_z = z_cur.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_x(z_to_x(z_cur))
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 1e10

    pos = 0
    n_betas = int(len(betas))
    for beta in betas:
        rem = float(deadline) - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 20.0:
            break

        remaining_count = max(1, n_betas - pos)
        sub_dead = time.monotonic() + (0.95 * rem) / float(remaining_count)
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, float(deadline) - 3.0)
        if sub_dead < time.monotonic() + 12.0:
            sub_dead = time.monotonic() + 12.0

        # Restart each beta from the best exact sequence.
        z_cur = best_z.copy()
        calls = 0
        while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 3.0 and calls < 3:
            rem_s = sub_dead - time.monotonic()
            if rem_s < 8.0:
                break
            mf = int((0.5 * rem_s) / max(est, 1e-5))
            mf = max(20, min(1000, mf))
            sc, z2 = run_lbfgs_z(z_cur, float(beta), sub_dead, mf, est)
            calls += 1

            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_z = z2.copy()
                if sc <= best_sc + 0.005:
                    z_cur = z2.copy()
                else:
                    z_cur = best_z.copy()
            else:
                z_cur = best_z.copy()

        pos += 1

    print("{} short lbfgs best={:.8f}".format(label, best_sc))
    return float(best_sc), best_z


def active_weights(x, n, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = fast_self_conv(x)
    d = float(n) * c
    if d.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(d.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(d.size)
    zeta = beta * (d - dmax)
    zeta = np.clip(zeta, -745.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(zeta)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(d.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(d.size)
    return e / s


def flat_value_grad_z(z, w):
    """
    Objective to make h = B_w x as flat as possible on entries.
    This approximates the KKT condition for active constraints.
    """
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(z.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    x = z_to_x(z)

    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size != fast_self_conv(x).size:
        w = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64) / float(2 * n - 1)

    h = corr_pair(x, w)
    if h.size == 0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    mu = float(np.mean(h))
    r = h - mu
    br = corr_pair(r, w)

    scale = 1000.0 * float(n) * float(n)
    val = scale * float(np.mean(r * r))
    dval = scale * (4.0 / float(h.size)) * br

    dval = np.nan_to_num(dval, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    xt = float(np.dot(x, dval))
    if not np.isfinite(xt):
        xt = 0.0

    grad = x * (dval - xt)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    gm = float(np.mean(grad))
    if np.isfinite(gm):
        grad = grad - gm

    return float(val), grad


def run_flat_step(zstart, n, deadline, est, active_betas=(500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0)):
    z_cur = np.asarray(zstart, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    z_cur = np.nan_to_num(z_cur, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z_cur = z_cur - float(z_cur.mean())

    best_z = z_cur.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_x(z_to_x(z_cur))
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 1e10

    n_betas = int(len(active_betas))
    pos = 0
    for ab in active_betas:
        rem = float(deadline) - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 25.0:
            break

        remaining_count = max(1, n_betas - pos)
        budget = 0.55 * rem / float(remaining_count)
        if budget < 15.0:
            budget = max(15.0, rem - 10.0)

        x_cur = z_to_x(z_cur)
        w = active_weights(x_cur, n, float(ab))

        mf = int(budget / max(est, 1e-5))
        mf = max(20, min(350, mf))

        sub_dead = time.monotonic() + max(8.0, budget)
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, float(deadline) - 3.0)

        z2 = z_cur
        try:
            opts = {
                "maxfun": mf,
                "maxiter": max(50, 2 * mf),
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-9,
                "maxcor": 15,
                "maxls": 15,
                "disp": False,
            }
            res = minimize(flat_value_grad_z, z2, args=(w,),
                           method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, options=opts)
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                z2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            z2 = z_cur

        z2 = np.nan_to_num(z2, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        z2 = z2 - float(z2.mean())
        sc2 = exact_score_x(z_to_x(z2))

        if np.isfinite(sc2):
            if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = float(sc2)
                best_z = z2.copy()
            if sc2 <= best_sc + 0.002:
                z_cur = z2.copy()
            else:
                z_cur = best_z.copy()
        else:
            z_cur = best_z.copy()

        pos += 1

    print("flat active-step best={:.8f}".format(best_sc))
    return float(best_sc), best_z


def subgrad_polish_z(zstart, deadline, k_list=(1, 2, 3, 5, 8),
                     rate_list=(0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1)):
    z_cur = np.asarray(zstart, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    z_cur = np.nan_to_num(z_cur, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z_cur = z_cur - float(z_cur.mean())

    best_z = z_cur.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_x(z_to_x(z_cur))
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 1e10
    current_sc = best_sc

    n = int(z_cur.size)
    stale = 0
    it = 0

    while it < 80 and time.monotonic() < float(deadline):
        x = z_to_x(z_cur)
        c = fast_self_conv(x)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        d = float(n) * c
        dmax = float(np.max(d))
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
            break

        improved = False
        for k in k_list:
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
                break
            if k <= 0 or k >= d.size:
                w = np.ones(d.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(d.size)
            else:
                idx = np.argpartition(d, d.size - k)[d.size - k:]
                w = np.zeros(d.size, dtype=np.float64)
                w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)

            h = corr_pair(x, w)
            g = float(np.dot(w, c))
            grad = 4.0 * float(n) * x * (h - g)
            grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            gn = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
            if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn < 1e-12:
                continue
            step = grad / gn

            for eta in rate_list:
                if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
                    break
                z_trial = z_cur - float(eta) * step
                z_trial = np.nan_to_num(z_trial, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                z_trial = z_trial - float(z_trial.mean())
                x_trial = z_to_x(z_trial)
                sc = exact_score_x(x_trial)

                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_sc = float(sc)
                        best_z = z_trial.copy()
                        improved = True
                    if sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                        current_sc = float(sc)
                        z_cur = z_trial.copy()

        it += 1
        stale = 0 if improved else stale + 1
        if stale >= 3:
            break

    print("subgrad polish best={:.8f}".format(best_sc))
    return float(best_sc), best_z


def perturb_z(z, rng, strength=None):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = int(z.size)
    if n == 0:
        return z
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.12))

    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = z - float(z.mean())
    x = z_to_x(z)
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))

    if mode == 0:
        arr = x * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
        z_new = z_from_x(np.maximum(arr, 1e-12))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.20))
        arr = (1.0 - t) * x + t / float(n)
        z_new = z_from_x(arr)
    elif mode == 2:
        z_new = z[::-1].copy() + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif mode == 3:
        c = fast_self_conv(x)
        d = float(n) * c
        k = min(3, max(1, d.size))
        if k >= d.size:
            w = np.ones(d.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(d.size)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(d, d.size - k)[d.size - k:]
            w = np.zeros(d.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
        h = corr_pair(x, w)
        g = float(np.dot(w, c))
        grad = 4.0 * float(n) * x * (h - g)
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        gn = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if np.isfinite(gn) and gn > 1e-12:
            z_new = z - (0.25 * strength) * (grad / gn)
        else:
            z_new = z.copy()
    elif mode == 4:
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        klen = max(3, n // 500)
        if klen % 2 == 0:
            klen += 1
        if klen > n:
            klen = n if n % 2 == 1 else max(3, n - 1)
        if klen >= 3:
            c = klen // 2
            sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
            kern = np.exp(-(((np.arange(klen) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig)))
            kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
            noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
        noise -= float(noise.mean())
        z_new = z + (0.5 * strength) * noise
    else:
        arr = x * (1.0 + 0.10 * rng.standard_normal(n))
        z_new = z_from_x(np.maximum(arr, 1e-12))

    z_new = np.asarray(z_new, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z_new = np.nan_to_num(z_new, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z_new = z_new - float(z_new.mean())
    return z_new


def add_shape(arr, out, cap, n):
    if len(out) >= cap:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    if arr.size != n:
        arr = resample_sequence(arr, n)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    mn = float(np.min(arr))
    if mn < 0.0:
        arr = arr - mn + 0.05
    mx = float(np.max(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    x = normalize_a(arr)
    if x.size != n or np.any(np.isnan(x)):
        return
    out.append(x)


def make_initial_starts(n, parent, rng, cap=28):
    out = []
    n = int(n)

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        add_shape(parent, out, cap, n)
        add_shape(parent[::-1], out, cap, n)

    add_shape(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), out, cap, n)

    xg = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    pvals = (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0)
    qvals = (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0)
    for p in pvals:
        for q in qvals:
            arr = (xg + 1e-9) ** p * ((1.0 - xg) + 1e-9) ** q
            add_shape(arr, out, cap, n)
            if len(out) >= cap:
                break
        if len(out) >= cap:
            break

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            arr = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * xg + phase)
            add_shape(arr, out, cap, n)
            if len(out) >= cap:
                break
        if len(out) >= cap:
            break

    for amp in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5):
        add_shape(1.0 + amp * np.abs(2.0 * xg - 1.0), out, cap, n)
        add_shape(1.0 + amp * (2.0 * xg - 1.0) ** 2, out, cap, n)

    if len(out) < cap:
        Nf = next_power_of_two(n)
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
        for _ in range(10):
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(1.2, 3.5))
            power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** alpha)
            if power.size > 0:
                power[0] = 0.0
            phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=power.size)
            spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
            noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n].astype(np.float64)
            noise -= float(noise.mean())
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.5))
            arr = 0.5 + amp * noise
            add_shape(arr, out, cap, n)
            if len(out) >= cap:
                break

    if not out:
        out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    return out


def is_distinct(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != b.size or a.size == 0:
        return True
    return float(np.max(np.abs(a - b))) > 1e-4


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(300.0, float(budget_s) - 60.0)
    deadline = t_start + total
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 16:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        psize = int(parent.size)
        if 8192 <= psize <= 10920:
            n_primary = psize
        elif psize < 8192:
            n_primary = 8192
        else:
            n_primary = 10240
    else:
        n_primary = 10240

    print("parent available: {}, primary n={}".format(parent is not None, n_primary))

    starts = make_initial_starts(n_primary, parent, rng, cap=28)
    cand = []
    for idx, x in enumerate(starts):
        sc = exact_score_a(x)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            cand.append((sc, idx, x))

    if not cand:
        uniforms = normalize_a(np.ones(n_primary, dtype=np.float64))
        sc = exact_score_a(uniforms)
        cand = [(sc, 0, uniforms)]

    cand.sort()

    selected = []
    seen = []
    for item in cand:
        x = item[2]
        ok = True
        for y in seen:
            if not is_distinct(x, y):
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            selected.append((float(item[0]), x))
            seen.append(x)
        if len(selected) >= 3:
            break

    # Convert selected x's to z variables and verify exact scores.
    top_candidates = []
    best_global_sc = np.inf
    best_global_n = n_primary
    best_global_z = None

    for sc, x in selected:
        z = z_from_x(x)
        zsc = exact_score_x(z_to_x(z))
        if not np.isfinite(zsc):
            zsc = sc
        top_candidates.append((float(zsc), z))
        if zsc < best_global_sc:
            best_global_sc = float(zsc)
            best_global_n = n_primary
            best_global_z = z

    if best_global_z is None:
        best_global_z = np.zeros(n_primary, dtype=np.float64)
        best_global_sc = exact_score_x(z_to_x(best_global_z))

    print("initial best={:.8f} length={}".format(best_global_sc, n_primary))

    # Calibrate runtime per LSE gradient.
    z_cal = best_global_z.copy()
    t_eval = time.monotonic()
    value_grad_lse_z(z_cal, 1000.0)
    value_grad_lse_z(z_cal, 100.0)
    est = max(0.04, min(0.3, (time.monotonic() - t_eval) / 2.0 * 1.2))
    est = max(est, estimate_eval_time(n_primary) * 0.6)
    print("estimated eval time={:.4f}s".format(est))

    # Phase 1: primary, top initial candidate.
    t1_end = t_start + 0.50 * total
    if t1_end < time.monotonic() + 60.0:
        t1_end = time.monotonic() + 60.0
    t1_end = min(t1_end, deadline - 30.0)

    if time.monotonic() < t1_end - 30.0 and len(top_candidates) > 0:
        stages = get_beta_stages(top_candidates[0][0])
        sc, z = optimize_lbfgs_trajectory(top_candidates[0][1], n_primary,
                                          t1_end, est, stages, "phaseA")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
            best_global_sc = float(sc)
            best_global_n = n_primary
            best_global_z = z

    # Phase 2: second candidate only if we still need improvement.
    if len(top_candidates) > 1 and best_global_sc > 1.5045:
        t2_end = t_start + 0.58 * total
        t2_end = min(t2_end, deadline - 240.0)
        if time.monotonic() < t2_end - 40.0:
            stages = get_beta_stages(top_candidates[1][0])
            sc, z = optimize_lbfgs_trajectory(top_candidates[1][1], n_primary,
                                              t2_end, est, stages, "phaseB")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
                best_global_sc = float(sc)
                best_global_n = n_primary
                best_global_z = z

    # Phase 3: third candidate if still far from target.
    if len(top_candidates) > 2 and best_global_sc > 1.5050:
        t3_end = t_start + 0.64 * total
        t3_end = min(t3_end, deadline - 240.0)
        if time.monotonic() < t3_end - 40.0:
            stages = get_beta_stages(top_candidates[2][0])
            sc, z = optimize_lbfgs_trajectory(top_candidates[2][1], n_primary,
                                              t3_end, est, stages, "phaseC")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
                best_global_sc = float(sc)
                best_global_n = n_primary
                best_global_z = z

    # Phase 4: active-set flatness polishing.
    t_flat_end = t_start + 0.72 * total
    t_flat_end = min(t_flat_end, deadline - 180.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_flat_end - 30.0:
        sc, z = run_flat_step(best_global_z, best_global_n, t_flat_end, est,
                              active_betas=(500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
            best_global_sc = float(sc)
            best_global_n = best_global_n
            best_global_z = z

    # Phase 5: exact top-k subgradient polish.
    t_poly_end = t_start + 0.80 * total
    t_poly_end = min(t_poly_end, deadline - 150.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_poly_end - 30.0:
        sc, z = subgrad_polish_z(best_global_z, t_poly_end,
                                 k_list=(1, 2, 3, 5, 8),
                                 rate_list=(0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
            best_global_sc = float(sc)
            best_global_n = best_global_n
            best_global_z = z

    # Phase 6: very high beta LSE continuation.
    t_high_end = t_start + 0.88 * total
    t_high_end = min(t_high_end, deadline - 120.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_high_end - 30.0:
        sc, z = short_lbfgs(best_global_z, best_global_n, t_high_end, est,
                            (200000.0, 800000.0, 2000000.0), "phase-high")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
            best_global_sc = float(sc)
            best_global_n = best_global_n
            best_global_z = z

    # Phase 7: perturb and re-optimize, to escape flat active sets.
    t_pert_end = t_start + 0.93 * total
    t_pert_end = min(t_pert_end, deadline - 90.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_pert_end - 45.0:
        remaining = t_pert_end - time.monotonic()
        attempts = 2
        for p in range(attempts):
            chunk = time.monotonic() + remaining / float(attempts - p) - 10.0
            chunk = min(chunk, deadline - 25.0)
            if chunk < time.monotonic() + 25.0:
                break
            pz = perturb_z(best_global_z, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.10)))
            sc, pz2 = short_lbfgs(pz, best_global_n, chunk, est,
                                  (5000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0),
                                  "perturb-{}".format(p))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
                best_global_sc = float(sc)
                best_global_n = best_global_n
                best_global_z = pz2.copy()

    # Phase 8: multi-length exploration.
    t_multi_end = t_start + 0.97 * total
    t_multi_end = min(t_multi_end, deadline - 60.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_multi_end - 45.0:
        best_x = z_to_x(best_global_z)
        n_list = []
        for val in (16384, 10920, 10240, 8192):
            if val != best_global_n and val not in n_list:
                n_list.append(val)

        used = 0
        for n2 in n_list:
            if used >= 2 or time.monotonic() > t_multi_end - 45.0:
                break
            x2 = resample_sequence(best_x, n2)
            z2 = z_from_x(x2)
            est2 = estimate_eval_time(n2) * 1.25
            sc, z2o = short_lbfgs(z2, n2, t_multi_end, est2,
                                  (5000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0),
                                  "multi-n={}".format(n2))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
                best_global_sc = float(sc)
                best_global_n = n2
                best_global_z = z2o
            used += 1

    # Final subgradient cleanup.
    t_final = deadline - 15.0
    if time.monotonic() < t_final - 25.0:
        sc, z = subgrad_polish_z(best_global_z, t_final,
                                 k_list=(1, 2, 3, 5),
                                 rate_list=(0.002, 0.005, 0.02))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_sc:
            best_global_sc = float(sc)
            best_global_n = best_global_n
            best_global_z = z

    out_arr = z_to_x(best_global_z)
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(S):
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(v) for v in out_arr]

    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float(best_global_sc) if np.isfinite(best_global_sc) else np.inf

    print("FINAL internal={:.8f} verified={:.8f} length={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        best_global_sc, verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start))
    return out
```
